\begin{split}&|q|\varepsilon_{\alpha\beta}\int dKdK^{\prime}E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}f_{0k}f_{0k^{\prime}}\Bigg[\int dPdP^{\prime}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}\delta^{4}(\cdots)(p^{\alpha}p^{\beta}+p^{\prime\alpha}p^{\prime\beta})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt-(k^{\alpha}k^{\beta}+k^{\prime\alpha}k^{\prime\beta})\int dPdP^{\prime}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}\delta^{4}(\cdots)\Bigg]\end{split}

(B7)

Using the known identity for the scattering kernel
[
32
,
31
]
:

\displaystyle\int dPdP^{\prime}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}\delta^{4}(\cdots)

\displaystyle=s\sigma_{T}

(B8)

\displaystyle\int dPdP^{\prime}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}\delta^{4}(\cdots)(p^{\alpha}p^{\beta}+p^{\prime\alpha}p^{\prime\beta})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3}\sigma_{T}s(k^{\alpha}+k^{\prime\alpha})(k^{\beta}+k^{\prime\beta})-\frac{1}{12}s^{2}\sigma_{T}g^{\alpha\beta},

(B9)

we substitute into the previous expression and perform the algebra:

\begin{split}&=\frac{4}{3}|q|\sigma_{T}\varepsilon_{\alpha\beta}\int dKdK^{\prime}E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\lambda}k^{\prime}_{\lambda}(k^{\alpha}k^{\beta}+k^{\prime\alpha}k^{\beta}+k^{\prime\beta}k^{\alpha}+k^{\prime\alpha}k^{\prime\beta})f_{0k}f_{0k^{\prime}}\\
&\quad-2|q|\sigma_{T}\varepsilon_{\alpha\beta}\int dKdK^{\prime}E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\lambda}k^{\prime}_{\lambda}(k^{\alpha}k^{\beta}+k^{\prime\alpha}k^{\prime\beta})f_{0k}f_{0k^{\prime}}\end{split}

(B10)

At this point, we evaluate each term using the definitions of the particle number density, equilibrium integrals, and moment decompositions in the Landau frame. After evaluating the contractions with
u^{\mu}
and
\Delta^{\mu\nu}
, and performing all simplifications, the full collision integral reduces to:

-\frac{8}{9}|q|\sigma_{T}n_{0}^{2}T^{2}\varepsilon_{\alpha\beta}(u^{\beta}\Delta^{\mu\alpha}+u^{\alpha}\Delta^{\mu\beta})

(B11)

Recognizing the linear combinations of the vector moments in the decomposition of
\delta h^{\mu}
, this yields:

-\frac{8}{9}|q|\sigma_{T}n_{0}^{2}T^{2}(2D_{3}V^{\mu}+2D_{4}h^{\mu})=-\frac{8}{9}|q|\sigma_{T}n_{0}(V^{\mu}-\frac{1}{4\,T}h^{\mu})

(B12)

where the second equality uses the known relations among the moment coefficients.

Finally for
V^{\mu}_{q}
the single particle contribution will look like:

=-\frac{4}{9}\sigma_{T}\left(n_{q}\,V^{\mu}+n\,V_{q}-|q|n\frac{\delta h^{\mu}}{4T}\right)

(B13)

The cross-species contribution to the collision integral simplifies significantly upon interchanging
p\leftrightarrow p^{\prime}
and using the symmetry
\sigma_{T}^{+-}=\sigma_{T}^{-+}
. Starting from the antisymmetric combination of gain and loss terms:

|q|\int dK\,dK^{\prime}\,dP\,dP^{\prime}\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}^{+-}\delta^{4}(p+p^{\prime}-k-k^{\prime})\left(f_{p}^{+}f_{p^{\prime}}^{-}-f_{k}^{+}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}-f_{p}^{+}f_{p^{\prime}}^{-}+f_{k}^{-}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}\right)\\
=|q|\int dK\,dK^{\prime}\,dP\,dP^{\prime}\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}^{+-}\delta^{4}(p+p^{\prime}-k-k^{\prime})\left(f_{k}^{-}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}-f_{k}^{+}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}\right)

(B14)

This expression separates into two terms involving moments of the distribution functions:

=2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\nu}f_{k}^{-}\int dK^{\prime}\,k^{\prime}_{\nu}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}-2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\nu}f_{k}^{+}\int dK^{\prime}\,k^{\prime}_{\nu}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}

(B15)

Decomposing the momentum products in terms of fluid velocity and spatial projectors yields:

=2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}\left(k^{\langle\mu}k^{\nu\rangle}-\frac{E_{k}^{2}}{3}\Delta^{\mu\nu}+E_{k}\,k^{\langle\mu\rangle}u^{\nu}\right)f_{k}^{-}\int dK^{\prime}\,\left(k^{\prime}_{\langle\nu\rangle}+u_{\nu}E_{k^{\prime}}\right)f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}\\
-2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}\left(k^{\langle\mu}k^{\nu\rangle}-\frac{E_{k}^{2}}{3}\Delta^{\mu\nu}+E_{k}\,k^{\langle\mu\rangle}u^{\nu}\right)f_{k}^{+}\int dK^{\prime}\,\left(k^{\prime}_{\langle\nu\rangle}+u_{\nu}E_{k^{\prime}}\right)f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}

(B16)

Identifying the relevant moments of the distribution functions as hydrodynamic fields, we express the result compactly as:

=2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\left(\gamma^{\pi}_{-1}\left(\pi^{\mu\nu}_{-}V^{+}_{\nu}-\pi^{\mu\nu}_{+}V^{-}_{\nu}\right)+\frac{4}{3}\left(n^{+}V^{\mu}_{-}-n^{-}V^{\mu}_{+}\right)\right)

(B17)

In the linearized limit, expressing in terms of the net-charge and total particle diffusion currents:

=\frac{4}{3}\sigma_{T}^{+-}\left(n_{q}\,V^{\mu}-n\,V_{q}^{\mu}\right)

(B18)

B.3
Collision term for
V^{\mu}

The single–particle contribution to the collision term, obtained from Eq. (
B12
), can be expressed for
V^{\mu}
as

=-\frac{4}{9}\sigma_{T}\left(nV^{\mu}+\frac{V_{q}}{|q|^{2}}n_{q}-\frac{\delta h^{\mu}}{4|q|T}n_{q}\right)

(B19)

For the cross–species contribution, we follow the same steps as for
V_{q}^{\mu}
and write the corresponding integral as

|q|\int dK\,dK^{\prime}\,dP\,dP^{\prime}\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}^{+-}\delta^{4}(p+p^{\prime}-k-k^{\prime})\left(f_{p}^{+}f_{p^{\prime}}^{-}-f_{k}^{+}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}+f_{p}^{+}f_{p^{\prime}}^{-}-f_{k}^{-}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}\right)\\
=|q|\int dK\,dK^{\prime}\,dP\,dP^{\prime}\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}(2\pi)^{5}s\sigma_{T}^{+-}\delta^{4}(p+p^{\prime}-k-k^{\prime})\left(2f_{p}^{+}f_{p^{\prime}}^{-}-f_{k}^{-}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}-f_{k}^{+}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}\right)

(B20)

This expression separates naturally into three integrals:

\begin{split}&=2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dKE_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k_{\nu}sW^{+-}_{kk^{\prime}\leftrightarrow pp^{\prime}}f_{0k}^{+}f_{0k^{\prime}}^{-}\left(\varphi_{p}^{+}+\varphi_{p}^{\prime-}\right)\\
&\quad-2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\nu}f_{k}^{-}\int dK^{\prime}\,k^{\prime}_{\nu}f_{k^{\prime}}^{+}-2|q|\sigma_{T}^{+-}\int dK\,E_{k}^{-1}k^{\langle\mu\rangle}k^{\nu}f_{k}^{+}\int dK^{\prime}\,k^{\prime}_{\nu}f_{k^{\prime}}^{-}\end{split}

(B21)

In these expressions, we have used the linearized expansion of the single–particle distribution functions and the equilibrium identity

f_{0k}^{\pm}f_{0k^{\prime}}^{\mp}=f_{0p}^{\pm}f_{0p^{\prime}}^{\mp}.

Substituting the explicit forms of
\varphi
from Eqs. (
A2
) and (
A3
), and evaluating the corresponding moment integrals analogous to those for the net charge diffusion four–current, we obtain the following compact form for the collision integral of the net charge four–current:

-\frac{2}{9}\sigma_{T}^{+-}nV^{\mu}+\frac{2}{9}\sigma_{T}^{+-}\frac{V^{\mu}_{q}}{|q|^{2}}n_{q}+\frac{8}{9}\sigma_{T}^{+-}\delta h^{\mu}\frac{n_{q}}{|q|T}

(B22)
